# Real-dataset plans

Generated by `bench/report.mjs`. Compiled SQL and `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` were captured on the deployed DO; `est` is the product of `sqlite_stat1` equality fan-outs along the plan (no filter selectivity), `actual` is a `COUNT(*)` of the join prefix run on the same DO.

## bsbm1m · B-Q1a

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm-inst: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?product ?label
WHERE {
    ?product rdfs:label ?label .
    ?product a <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductType66> .
    ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature3> .
    ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature1967> .
    ?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?value1 .
	FILTER (?value1 > 136)
	}
ORDER BY ?label
LIMIT 10
```

## bsbm1m · B-Q3a

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm-inst: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT ?product ?label
WHERE {
    ?product rdfs:label ?label .
    ?product a <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductType119> .
	?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature69> .
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?p1 .
	FILTER ( ?p1 > 34 )
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric3 ?p3 .
	FILTER (?p3 < 44 )
    OPTIONAL {
        ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature3728> .
        ?product rdfs:label ?testVar }
    FILTER (!bound(?testVar))
}
ORDER BY ?label
LIMIT 10
```

```sql
SELECT r."product" AS c0, t0.kind AS k0, t0.lex AS l0, t0.dt AS d0, t0.lang AS g0, r."label" AS c1, t1.kind AS k1, t1.lex AS l1, t1.dt AS d1, t1.lang AS g1 FROM (SELECT q0.s AS "product", q0.o AS "label", (SELECT CASE kind WHEN 1 THEN 1 WHEN 0 THEN 2 ELSE 3 END FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) AS "_o0", (SELECT num FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) AS "_o1", (SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) AS "_o2" FROM quads AS q0 JOIN quads AS q1 JOIN quads AS q2 JOIN quads AS q3 JOIN quads AS q4 LEFT JOIN (SELECT q5.s AS "product", q6.o AS "testVar" FROM quads AS q5 JOIN quads AS q6 WHERE (likelihood(q5.p = 14740, 5.951e-2)) AND (likelihood(q5.o = 11662, 1.750e-5)) AND (q5.s = q6.s) AND (likelihood(q6.p = 4, 8.094e-3))) AS o7 ON (q0.s = o7."product") WHERE (likelihood(q0.p = 4, 8.094e-3)) AND (q0.s = q1.s) AND (likelihood(q1.p = 2, 1.042e-1)) AND (likelihood(q1.o = 396, 1.500e-4)) AND (q0.s = q2.s) AND (likelihood(q2.p = 14740, 5.951e-2)) AND (likelihood(q2.o = 700, 1.785e-3)) AND (q0.s = q3.s) AND (likelihood(q3.p = 14724, 2.901e-3)) AND (q0.s = q4.s) AND (likelihood(q4.p = 14728, 2.901e-3)) AND (((SELECT num FROM terms WHERE id = q3.o AND kind = 4) > 34)) AND (((SELECT num FROM terms WHERE id = q4.o AND kind = 4) < 44)) AND ((NOT (o7."testVar" IS NOT NULL))) ORDER BY (SELECT CASE kind WHEN 1 THEN 1 WHEN 0 THEN 2 ELSE 3 END FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o), (SELECT num FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o), (SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) LIMIT 10) r LEFT JOIN main.terms t0 ON t0.id = r."product" LEFT JOIN main.terms t1 ON t1.id = r."label" ORDER BY r."_o0", r."_o1", r."_o2"
```

## bsbm1m · B-Q4a

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm-inst: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?product ?label ?propertyTextual
WHERE {
    {
       ?product rdfs:label ?label .
       ?product rdf:type <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductType119> .
       ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature3728> .
	   ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature56> .
       ?product bsbm:productPropertyTextual1 ?propertyTextual .
	   ?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?p1 .
	   FILTER ( ?p1 > 159 )
    } UNION {
       ?product rdfs:label ?label .
       ?product rdf:type <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductType119> .
       ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature3728> .
	   ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature1052> .
       ?product bsbm:productPropertyTextual1 ?propertyTextual .
	   ?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric2 ?p2 .
	   FILTER ( ?p2> 25 )
    }
}
ORDER BY ?label
OFFSET 5
LIMIT 10
```

## bsbm1m · B-Q5a

```sparql
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?product ?productLabel
WHERE {
	?product rdfs:label ?productLabel .
    FILTER (<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer49/Product2288> != ?product)
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer49/Product2288> bsbm:productFeature ?prodFeature .
	?product bsbm:productFeature ?prodFeature .
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer49/Product2288> bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?origProperty1 .
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?simProperty1 .
	FILTER (?simProperty1 < (?origProperty1 + 120) && ?simProperty1 > (?origProperty1 - 120))
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer49/Product2288> bsbm:productPropertyNumeric2 ?origProperty2 .
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric2 ?simProperty2 .
	FILTER (?simProperty2 < (?origProperty2 + 170) && ?simProperty2 > (?origProperty2 - 170))
}
ORDER BY ?productLabel
LIMIT 5
```

## bsbm1m · B-Q6a

```sparql
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>

SELECT ?product ?label
WHERE {
	?product rdfs:label ?label .
    ?product rdf:type bsbm:Product .
	FILTER regex(?label, "functionalistic")
}
```

```sparql
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rev: <http://purl.org/stuff/rev#>

PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>

SELECT ?productLabel ?offer ?price ?vendor ?vendorTitle ?review ?revTitle
       ?reviewer ?revName ?rating1 ?rating2
WHERE {
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer35/Product1561> rdfs:label ?productLabel .
    OPTIONAL {
        ?offer bsbm:product <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer35/Product1561> .
		?offer bsbm:price ?price .
		?offer bsbm:vendor ?vendor .
		?vendor rdfs:label ?vendorTitle .
        ?vendor bsbm:country <http://downlode.org/rdf/iso-3166/countries#DE> .
        ?offer dc:publisher ?vendor .
        ?offer bsbm:validTo ?date .
        FILTER (?date > "2008-06-20T00:00:00"^^<http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#dateTime> )
    }
    OPTIONAL {
	?review bsbm:reviewFor <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer35/Product1561> .
	?review rev:reviewer ?reviewer .
	?reviewer foaf:name ?revName .
	?review dc:title ?revTitle .
    OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating1 ?rating1 . }
    OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating2 ?rating2 . }
    }
}
```

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
PREFIX rev: <http://purl.org/stuff/rev#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?title ?text ?reviewDate ?reviewer ?reviewerName ?rating1 ?rating2 ?rating3 ?rating4
WHERE {
	?review bsbm:reviewFor <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer41/Product1919> .
	?review dc:title ?title .
	?review rev:text ?text .
	FILTER langMatches( lang(?text), "EN" )
	?review bsbm:reviewDate ?reviewDate .
	?review rev:reviewer ?reviewer .
	?reviewer foaf:name ?reviewerName .
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating1 ?rating1 . }
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating2 ?rating2 . }
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating3 ?rating3 . }
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating4 ?rating4 . }
}
ORDER BY DESC(?reviewDate)
LIMIT 20
```

## bsbm5m · B-Q3a

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm-inst: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT ?product ?label
WHERE {
    ?product rdfs:label ?label .
    ?product a <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductType303> .
	?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature9693> .
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?p1 .
	FILTER ( ?p1 > 34 )
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric3 ?p3 .
	FILTER (?p3 < 44 )
    OPTIONAL {
        ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature165> .
        ?product rdfs:label ?testVar }
    FILTER (!bound(?testVar))
}
ORDER BY ?label
LIMIT 10
```

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm-inst: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>

       ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature2087> .
	   ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature9860> .
       ?product bsbm:productPropertyTextual1 ?propertyTextual .
	   ?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?p1 .

    } UNION {
       ?product rdfs:label ?label .
       ?product rdf:type <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductType308> .
       ?product bsbm:productFeature <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/ProductFeature2087> .

       ?product bsbm:productPropertyTextual1 ?propertyTextual .
	   ?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric2 ?p2 .
	   FILTER ( ?p2> 25 )
    }
}
ORDER BY ?label
OFFSET 5
LIMIT 10
```

## bsbm5m · B-Q5a

```sparql
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?product ?productLabel
WHERE {
	?product rdfs:label ?productLabel .
    FILTER (<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer118/Product5553> != ?product)
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer118/Product5553> bsbm:productFeature ?prodFeature .
	?product bsbm:productFeature ?prodFeature .
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer118/Product5553> bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?origProperty1 .
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric1 ?simProperty1 .
	FILTER (?simProperty1 < (?origProperty1 + 120) && ?simProperty1 > (?origProperty1 - 120))
	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer118/Product5553> bsbm:productPropertyNumeric2 ?origProperty2 .
	?product bsbm:productPropertyNumeric2 ?simProperty2 .
	FILTER (?simProperty2 < (?origProperty2 + 170) && ?simProperty2 > (?origProperty2 - 170))
}
ORDER BY ?productLabel
LIMIT 5
```

## bsbm5m · B-Q6a

```sparql
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>

SELECT ?product ?label
WHERE {
	?product rdfs:label ?label .
    ?product rdf:type bsbm:Product .
	FILTER regex(?label, "antagonistically")
}
```

```sparql
PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>

       ?reviewer ?revName ?rating1 ?rating2

	<http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer29/Product1326> rdfs:label ?productLabel .
    OPTIONAL {
        ?offer bsbm:product <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer29/Product1326> .

		?offer bsbm:vendor ?vendor .
		?vendor rdfs:label ?vendorTitle .
        ?vendor bsbm:country <http://downlode.org/rdf/iso-3166/countries#DE> .
        ?offer dc:publisher ?vendor .

	?review bsbm:reviewFor <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer29/Product1326> .
	?review rev:reviewer ?reviewer .
	?reviewer foaf:name ?revName .
	?review dc:title ?revTitle .
    OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating1 ?rating1 . }
    OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating2 ?rating2 . }
    }
}
```

## bsbm5m · B-Q8a

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
PREFIX rev: <http://purl.org/stuff/rev#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?title ?text ?reviewDate ?reviewer ?reviewerName ?rating1 ?rating2 ?rating3 ?rating4
WHERE {
	?review bsbm:reviewFor <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer77/Product3649> .
	?review dc:title ?title .
	?review rev:text ?text .
	FILTER langMatches( lang(?text), "EN" )
	?review bsbm:reviewDate ?reviewDate .
	?review rev:reviewer ?reviewer .
	?reviewer foaf:name ?reviewerName .
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating1 ?rating1 . }
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating2 ?rating2 . }
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating3 ?rating3 . }
	OPTIONAL { ?review bsbm:rating4 ?rating4 . }
}
ORDER BY DESC(?reviewDate)
LIMIT 20
```

```sparql
PREFIX bsbm: <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/vocabulary/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?offer ?price
WHERE {
	?offer bsbm:product <http://www4.wiwiss.fu-berlin.de/bizer/bsbm/v01/instances/dataFromProducer276/Product13557> .
	?offer bsbm:vendor ?vendor .
    ?offer dc:publisher ?vendor .
	?vendor bsbm:country <http://downlode.org/rdf/iso-3166/countries#US> .
	?offer bsbm:deliveryDays ?deliveryDays .
	FILTER (?deliveryDays <= 3)
	?offer bsbm:price ?price .
    ?offer bsbm:validTo ?date .
    FILTER (?date > "2008-06-20T00:00:00"^^<http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#dateTime> )
}
ORDER BY xsd:double(str(?price))
LIMIT 10
```

```sparql
PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
SELECT ?p ?title ?year WHERE { ?p dblp:authoredBy <https://dblp.org/pid/97/5026> ; dblp:title ?title ; dblp:yearOfPublication ?year } ORDER BY DESC(?year)
```

```sql

```

## dblp6m · D9-text-year

```sparql
PREFIX dblp: <https://dblp.org/rdf/schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
SELECT ?p ?title ?year WHERE { ?p dblp:title ?title ; dblp:yearOfPublication ?year . FILTER (xsd:integer(STR(?year)) >= 2020 && REGEX(?title, "^Deep", "i")) } ORDER BY ?title LIMIT 50
```

```sql
SELECT r."p" AS c0, t0.kind AS k0, t0.lex AS l0, t0.dt AS d0, t0.lang AS g0, r."title" AS c1, t1.kind AS k1, t1.lex AS l1, t1.dt AS d1, t1.lang AS g1, r."year" AS c2, t2.kind AS k2, t2.lex AS l2, t2.dt AS d2, t2.lang AS g2 FROM (SELECT q0.s AS "p", q0.o AS "title", q1.o AS "year", (SELECT CASE kind WHEN 1 THEN 1 WHEN 0 THEN 2 ELSE 3 END FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) AS "_o0", (SELECT num FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) AS "_o1", (SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) AS "_o2" FROM quads AS q0 JOIN quads AS q1 WHERE (likelihood(q0.p = 22, 1.933e-2)) AND (q0.s = q1.s) AND (likelihood(q1.p = 37, 1.932e-2)) AND (((CAST((CASE WHEN (length(trim((SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q1.o AND kind <> 1))) - length(ltrim(trim((SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q1.o AND kind <> 1)), '+-')) <= 1 AND ltrim(trim((SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q1.o AND kind <> 1)), '+-') GLOB '*[0-9]*' AND NOT ltrim(trim((SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q1.o AND kind <> 1)), '+-') GLOB '*[^0-9]*') THEN CAST(trim((SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q1.o AND kind <> 1)) AS REAL) END) AS INTEGER) >= 2020) AND ((SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o AND kind IN (2, 3)) LIKE 'Deep%' ESCAPE '\'))) ORDER BY (SELECT CASE kind WHEN 1 THEN 1 WHEN 0 THEN 2 ELSE 3 END FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o), (SELECT num FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o), (SELECT lex FROM terms WHERE id = q0.o) LIMIT 50) r LEFT JOIN main.terms t0 ON t0.id = r."p" LEFT JOIN main.terms t1 ON t1.id = r."title" LEFT JOIN main.terms t2 ON t2.id = r."year" ORDER BY r."_o0", r."_o1", r."_o2"
```

```sql
SELECT r."a" AS c0, t0.kind AS k0, t0.lex AS l0, t0.dt AS d0, t0.lang AS g0, r."n" AS c1 FROM (SELECT a2."a" AS "a", a2."_a0" AS "n", a2."_a0" AS "_o0" FROM (SELECT q1.o AS "a", COUNT(DISTINCT q0.s) AS "_a0" FROM quads AS q0 JOIN quads AS q1 WHERE (likelihood(q0.p = 37, 1.932e-2)) AND (likelihood(q0.o = 147298, 3.829e-2)) AND (q0.s = q1.s) AND (likelihood(q1.p = 48, 1.953e-2)) GROUP BY q1.o) AS a2 ORDER BY a2."_a0" DESC LIMIT 10) r LEFT JOIN main.terms t0 ON t0.id = r."a" ORDER BY r."_o0" DESC
```

```
2/0 CO-ROUTINE r
4/2 CO-ROUTINE a2
11/4 SEARCH q0 USING COVERING INDEX quads_pos (p=? AND o=?)
16/4 SEARCH q1 USING PRIMARY KEY (s=? AND p=?)
22/4 USE TEMP B-TREE FOR GROUP BY
61/4 USE TEMP B-TREE FOR count(DISTINCT)
67/2 SCAN a2
81/2 USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
91/0 SCAN r
94/0 SEARCH t0 USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
```

| SEARCH q0 USING COVERING INDEX quads_pos (p=? AND o=?) |   3 |   3788 |
| SEARCH q1 USING PRIMARY KEY (s=? AND p=?)              |   6 |  15847 |

## dblp6m · D16-regex-quantified

```sparql
PREFIX dblp: <https://dblp.org/rdf/schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
SELECT ?p ?title WHERE { ?p dblp:title ?title . FILTER REGEX(?title, "^(deep|neural) [a-z]+ networks?", "i") } LIMIT 100
```

## dblp6m · D17-datatype

```sparql
PREFIX dblp: <https://dblp.org/rdf/schema#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
SELECT ?dt (COUNT(*) AS ?n) WHERE { ?p dblp:yearOfPublication ?y . BIND (DATATYPE(?y) AS ?dt) } GROUP BY ?dt
```
